Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SHOW框架，通过统一度量空间耦合3D场景重建与人体网格恢复，解决单目动态场景中尺度模糊、人体-场景对齐及遮挡问题，显著提升重建精度与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景中单目相机重建存在尺度模糊、人体-场景空间错位及遮挡干扰等挑战，现有方法将人体与场景重建分离处理，缺乏跨任务语义与几何约束的协同优化。

Method: 构建SHOW框架：1) 将参数化人体模型的语义结构与度量尺度先验注入归一化点云预测；2) 利用场景几何约束人体网格估计；3) 引入提示掩码机制实现目标人体选择与背景抑制；4) 联合训练学习人体感知几何特征与几何约束人体特征。

Result: 在复杂相机运动、遮挡及杂乱背景下，SHOW显著提升度量尺度一致性、人体-场景对齐精度及重建准确率，验证了统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: SHOW通过人体与场景重建的相互增强机制，在单目视频输入下实现高精度度量级重建，为动态场景理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing humans in dynamic scenes from moving monocular cameras remains challenging due to scale ambiguity, human-scene misalignment, and occlusion interference. Rather than treating human mesh recovery and scene reconstruction as separate tasks, we believe that accurate human-scene reconstruction requires the two tasks to mutually inform each other: parametric human models offer semantic structure and metric-scale priors, while scene geometry provides spatial context for human localization and alignment. Built on this insight, we introduce SHOW, a mask-promptable human mesh recovery framework that couples feed-forward 3D scene reconstruction with Human Mesh Recovery in a unified metric space. SHOW injects human semantics and scale priors from parametric human models into normalized point-map prediction, enabling metric-scale scene reconstruction from inherently scale-ambiguous monocular input. In turn, the recovered scene geometry constrains human mesh estimation, encouraging spatially consistent human placement and improved human-scene alignment. To handle complex multi-person and cluttered scenes, SHOW further incorporates a promptable masking mechanism that enables flexible target-human selection while suppressing background distractions and occlusion interference. Through joint training, the model learns both human-aware geometric features and geometry-constrained human features, producing aligned metric-scale reconstructions from monocular human-centric videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SHOW improves metric-scale consistency, human-scene alignment, and reconstruction accuracy under challenging camera motion, occlusion, and cluttered backgrounds.

</details>


### [39] [Learning 1-Bit LiDAR-based Localization with Auxiliary Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27729)
*Kaijie Yin,Zhiyuan Zhang,Tian Gao,Wentao Zhu,Cheng-zhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BiLoc是首个用于6-DoF LiDAR定位的二进制神经网络框架，通过信息瓶颈原理和自适应辅助目标缓解二值化信息损失，在大规模户外数据集上实现新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习定位方法计算开销大，难以满足车载资源限制；二进制神经网络虽高效但二值化导致严重信息损失和性能下降。

Method: 从信息瓶颈视角重构BNN训练，设计自适应辅助目标调节二进制编码器信息保留，抑制冗余并补偿表示能力不足与梯度失配问题。

Result: 在大规模户外LiDAR数据集上验证，BiLoc在保持极低计算成本的同时达到LiDAR定位新SOTA性能。

Conclusion: BiLoc首次实现高效二进制神经网络在6-DoF LiDAR定位的成功应用，为资源受限场景提供了兼具精度与效率的解决方案。

Abstract: 6-DoF LiDAR-based localization is a fundamental capability for autonomous systems operating in large-scale outdoor environments. Many deep-learning-based localization methods have achieved promising performance so far. However, as one of the always-on modules competing for limited on-board computational resources, the localization module is expected to consume only a small portion of the overall compute budget. Most existing learning-based methods are still too heavy for this purpose. In contrast, binary neural networks (BNNs) offer an appealing solution, but the 1-bit compression causes severe information loss and performance drop. In this paper, we address this challenge by proposing Binarized LiDAR-based Localization (BiLoc), the first binary neural network framework for 6-DoF LiDAR localization. Specifically, we reinterpret the training of BNNs from the perspective of the information-bottleneck principle, aiming at retaining minimal yet sufficient representations for pose estimation while suppressing redundant variations. And we introduce an auxiliary objective that adaptively regulates information retention in the binary encoder, effectively mitigating the information loss caused by binarization. This auxiliary objective provides additional optimization signals that compensate for the limited representational capacity and the gradient mismatch inherent in BNNs. Extensive experiments on large-scale outdoor LiDAR datasets demonstrate that BiLoc establishes a new state of the art for LiDAR localization with BNNs.

</details>


### [40] [SIFT: Self-Imagination Fine-Tuning for Physically Plausible Motion in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27741)
*Ruoyu Wang,Jialun Liu,Huayang Huang,Haibin Huang,Jiepeng Wang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li,Yu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对视频扩散模型中因数据偏差和重建训练导致的动作纠缠问题，提出自我想象微调（SIFT）范式，通过自生成数据训练、运动感知判别监督及渐进式困难案例重放策略，显著提升生成视频的物理真实性与动作解耦能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型虽提升视觉保真度，但生成动作常违反物理合理性，表现为“动作纠缠”（如相机运动与物体运动意外耦合）。该问题源于训练数据中的运动线索残留及重建导向的设计，导致模型依赖复制现有运动而非学习运动学原理。

Method: 提出SIFT范式，使模型通过自生成视频学习而非直接重建真实数据，打破重建捷径；结合运动感知判别监督与渐进式困难案例重放策略稳定训练；利用文本提示生成多样化运动场景，覆盖稀有/精细解耦案例。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在物理真实性、动作解耦性和可控性上取得显著提升，有效解决传统扩散模型的动作纠缠问题。

Conclusion: SIFT通过自监督学习与运动感知优化，为视频生成模型提供了更可靠的运动建模方案，推动生成内容向物理合理性和可控性方向发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have greatly improved visual fidelity, yet their generated motions often violate physical plausibility. We observe a common kinematic failure, "motion entanglement", the unintended coupling of independent motion sources, such as camera movement and object motion. We identify that this issue stems from data bias and the reconstruction-based training design of diffusion models. Training on noisy videos that still retain coarse motion cues inadvertently encourages the model to replicate existing motion without an incentive to learn how to model kinematically-grounded motions. To address this, we propose a Self-Imagination Fine-Tuning (SIFT) paradigm, which enables the model to learn from its own generated videos rather than directly reconstructing real ones, breaking the reconstruction shortcut. We further employ motion-aware discriminative supervision and a progressive hard-case replay strategy to stabilize and accelerate learning. By leveraging freely-generated text prompts, our method can densely cover a broad motion space, including rare or finely-disentangled scenarios that would be costly to collect as video data. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach substantially improves the physical realism, motion disentanglement, and controllability of generated videos.

</details>


### [41] [Panoramic Scene Analysis: A Survey from Distortion-Aware Engineering to Sphere-Native Foundation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27745)
*Qinfeng Zhu,Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述全景图像分析方法的演进，指出从投影适配到球体原生建模的几何深化趋势，揭示现有方法无法同时实现严格球面对称性和预训练权重复用，并提出评估协议改进方案及研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图像虽能捕获完整视觉空间，但其2球面到平面的映射必然引入几何失真，破坏传统视觉架构假设，亟需系统性解决几何适配与模型泛化问题。

Method: 沿架构设计（算子与球面几何交互方式）和训练范式（跨域知识迁移）双维度组织文献，覆盖密集预测、多任务理解、开放世界感知等任务，对比分析投影适配、失真感知工程、球体原生建模等技术路径。

Result: 发现现有方法均无法同时满足严格球面对称性与视角预训练权重复用，论证此为结构性缺陷；识别评估协议五大系统性缺失：球面面积加权指标、接缝一致性测试、极区鲁棒性分层、跨投影泛化验证及开放世界协议标准化。

Conclusion: 提出六点研究路线图推动通用全景智能发展，强调需突破几何约束与模型复用的根本矛盾，建立标准化评估体系，相关资源已开源。

Abstract: Panoramic images capture the complete visual sphere in a single frame, providing spatial context unattainable by conventional cameras. Yet this completeness comes at a geometric cost: the 2-sphere cannot be faithfully mapped to the plane, and every planar representation introduces distortions that violate the assumptions underlying standard vision architectures. This survey traces the evolution of panoramic scene analysis along a methodological trajectory, from projection-based adaptation, through distortion-aware engineering, to sphere-native modeling and geometry-aware tokenization for foundation models, and argues that this evolution reflects a progressive deepening of geometric commitment rather than a simple accumulation of techniques. We organize the literature along two orthogonal dimensions: architectural design (how operators interact with spherical geometry) and training paradigm (how knowledge is transferred across domains). Covering dense prediction (semantic segmentation, depth estimation, and room layout estimation), unified multi-task understanding, open-world perception, vision-language reasoning, and dynamic video analysis, we identify a central unresolved tension: among the methods surveyed, none simultaneously delivers strict spherical equivariance and full reuse of perspective-pretrained foundation-model weights, and we argue that this is a structural rather than incidental gap. We further expose five systematic gaps in current evaluation protocols, namely the absence of spherical-area-weighted metrics, seam-consistency testing, polar-robustness stratification, cross-projection generalization, and open-world protocol standardization, and propose a six-point research roadmap toward general-purpose panoramic intelligence. The corresponding repository is publicly available at: https://github.com/zhuqinfeng1999/Awesome-Panoramic-Scene-Analysis.

</details>


### [42] [PixelU: A U-Shaped Transformer for Efficient End-to-End Pixel Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27760)
*Zipeng Guo,Lichen Ma,Yu He,Xiaolong Fu,Jingling Fu,Junshi Huang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PixelU：一种极简单阶段U形扩散Transformer，通过零成本跳跃连接与恒定通道下采样机制，在像素空间中高效分离高频细节与低频语义建模，以1/3计算成本超越基线方法，在ImageNet上实现FID 1.63/1.92。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素空间扩散模型依赖复杂解码器解决高频信号建模难题，但作者发现这些解码器实为补偿速度预测(v-prediction)优化困难的冗余设计，在干净数据范式(x-prediction)下可被替代。

Method: 设计单阶段U形架构，采用零成本跳跃连接构建高频信息高速公路，结合恒定通道下采样作为低通滤波器压缩深层特征至低频语义流形，实现频率解耦建模。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256/512×512任务中取得FID 1.63/1.92，计算成本仅为JiT-G基线的1/3，性能超越现有像素空间方法。

Conclusion: 验证了频率解耦策略的有效性，确立了无需辅助解码器的端到端扩散模型新范式，证明简洁架构可通过结构创新实现性能突破。

Abstract: End-to-end pixel-space diffusion models bypass the lossy compression of Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) but struggle to jointly model low-frequency semantics and high-frequency signals in high-dimensional space. Existing works heavily rely on complex pixel decoders to alleviate this issue. In this paper, we challenge this trend by revealing that these decoders primarily compensate for the optimization difficulties inherent to velocity prediction ($v$-prediction). Under the clean data paradigm ($x$-prediction), they are redundant. Motivated by this insight, we advocate for simplicity over complexity and introduce PixelU, a minimalist, single-stage U-shaped Diffusion Transformer tailored for pixel space. PixelU abandons auxiliary decoders in favor of zero-cost skip connections, which provide an "information highway" that directly routes uncorrupted high-frequency spatial details from shallow to deep layers. To further enable the backbone to focus exclusively on modeling low-frequency semantics, we introduce a constant-channel spatial down-sampling mechanism as a natural low-pass filter, which compresses deep features into a compact, low-frequency semantic manifold. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this decoupling of frequencies could outperform the strong baseline (JiT-G) with only about 1/3 of its computation cost. On ImageNet 256$\times$256 and 512$\times$512, PixelU achieves FID of 1.63 and 1.92 respectively, surpassing recent pixel-space methods and establishing a simple yet powerful new paradigm for end-to-end diffusion models.

</details>


### [43] [Improving Adversarial Robustness via Activation Amplification and Attenuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27784)
*Taïga Gonçalves,Yongsong Huang,Tomo Miyazaki,Shinichiro Omachi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级插件模块A3，通过激活缩放机制动态放大/衰减对抗信号，显著提升对抗鲁棒性且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法（剪枝、掩码等）仅减少非鲁棒特征影响，而本工作探索联合学习放大与衰减对抗信号的潜力，以更高效地增强模型鲁棒性。

Method: 设计A3模块：利用可学习掩码和基于原始激活幅度的缩放因子动态重缩放激活值；通过翻转缩放符号实现信号放大/衰减；放大信号作为负参考构建对比与排序损失函数。

Result: 实验表明：放大模式下的预测退化学习同步提升衰减模式鲁棒性；仅需少量参数即可实现显著性能提升；在多种骨干网络、数据集及训练方法中均一致改善对抗鲁棒性，且计算/内存开销可忽略。

Conclusion: A3提供了一种高效、通用的对抗鲁棒性增强方案，其核心机制依赖激活缩放而非额外容量，为实际部署提供了低开销的解决方案。

Abstract: The existence of adversarial attacks is often attributed to the presence of non-robust features in neural networks. While prior defenses reduce their impact via pruning, masking, or feature recalibration, we instead propose to jointly learn to amplify and attenuate these signals through a simple activation scaling mechanism. To this end, we introduce Activation Amplification and Attenuation (A3), a lightweight plug-in module that enhances adversarial robustness with minimal modifications of the activations. A3 dynamically rescales the activations using a learnable mask and a scaling factor derived from the original activation magnitudes. The influence of adversarial perturbations can be amplified or attenuated using the same learnable parameters by simply flipping the sign of the scaling operation. The amplified signals serve as negative references to construct novel contrastive and ranking loss functions. Experimental analysis shows that learning to degrade the predictions in amplification mode simultaneously improves adversarial robustness in attenuation mode. Moreover, A3 relies on only a small number of learnable parameters, with most of its behavior being determined by the scaling mechanism rather than additional network capacity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that integrating A3 into different backbones, datasets, and training methods consistently improves adversarial robustness while introducing negligible computational and memory overhead compared to existing plug-in modules. Code is available at: https://github.com/tgoncalv/A3.

</details>


### [44] [ModaFlow: Modality-Aware Flow Matching for High-Fidelity Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27773)
*Xiangyu Sai,Meysam Madadi,Sergio Escalera,Yong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ModaFlow是一个基于流匹配的虚拟试衣框架，通过模态感知引导和正则化损失实现高保真度，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试衣方法难以同时保留服装细节并适应大变形下的人体几何差异，需解决多模态条件统一处理的问题。

Method: 提出模态感知引导机制：视觉嵌入提供结构约束，文本嵌入通过自适应CFG控制；设计余弦相似度和感知流判别损失优化流场；采用随机掩码策略增强泛化能力。

Result: 在配对/未配对基准测试中FID分别降低30%/20%，达到SOTA性能，显著提升生成质量与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ModaFlow通过模态解耦引导和流场正则化突破传统方法局限，为电商和AR应用提供高精度虚拟试衣解决方案。

Abstract: Image-based virtual try-on has emerged as a compelling task in e-commerce and augmented reality, yet existing methods struggle to simultaneously preserve fine garment semantics and adapt to diverse person body geometries under large clothing-body deformations. We present ModaFlow, a modality-aware flow-matching based framework for high-fidelity virtual try-on that achieves precise alignment between textual descriptions and garment appearance. Unlike prior methods that treat multimodal conditions uniformly, ModaFlow introduces a modality-aware guidance scheme: visual garment embeddings extracted by a pretrained image prompt adapter provide deterministic, persistent structural guidance, while textual embeddings generated from garment descriptions are controlled via classifier-free guidance (CFG) with adaptive scaling and zero-initialized velocity. To further enhance flow field accuracy, we propose two regularization losses, cosine similarity and perceptual flow discrimination, that jointly improve directional consistency and perceptual realism of the velocity field. Additionally, a mask manipulation strategy stochastically samples among box, transparent, and relaxed masks during training, simulating diverse occlusion scenarios and enabling robust inference under unpaired settings where only a box mask is available. Experiments show that ModaFlow achieves state-of-the-art results in both qualitative and quantitative evaluations, reducing FID by approximately 30% on paired and 20% on unpaired benchmarks.

</details>


### [45] [TRUST: Efficient Abdominal Trauma Recognition via Image-to-Ultrasound-Video Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27777)
*Enguang Wang,Hao Zhou,Shuo Gao,Tuo Liu,Guangquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRUST框架解决腹部超声视频分析中的扫描依赖性挑战，通过跨频率适配、多粒度运动建模和自适应视觉-文本对齐实现高效可靠的动态理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PEIVTL方法受限于医生操作差异导致的时空/语义变异，难以泛化至复杂超声扫描场景。

Method: 1) CFCA模块建立高低频约束增强空间特征提取；2) MGMA模块融合局部时序卷积与全局自注意力捕获扫描动态；3) VQSA模块生成条件化文本原型实现鲁棒视觉-文本对齐

Result: 在内部创伤数据集上较SOTA方法提升9.63%准确率，同时保持更高计算效率

Conclusion: TRUST通过显式建模细粒度时空变化，显著提升了超声视频理解的可靠性与泛化能力，为临床实时决策提供技术支撑

Abstract: Abdominal ultrasound is indispensable for rapid, noninvasive trauma triage. However, interpreting the subtle dynamic cues embedded in continuous scanning is time-intensive and operator-dependent. Parameter-Efficient Image-to-Video Transfer Learning (PEIVTL), which efficiently adapts pre-trained image models to the video domain, notably through visual-textual alignment, offers a promising paradigm for ultrasound video analysis. Nevertheless, substantial spatiotemporal and semantic variations arising from physician-dependent scanning practices continue to limit the effectiveness and generalizability of this framework. We propose TRUST, a scan-aware PEIVTL framework that explicitly models fine-grained spatiotemporal variations to enable reliable ultrasound video understanding. First, we introduce a Cross-Frequency Collaborative Adapter (CFCA) that establishes mutual constraints between low- and high-frequency components, enhancing discriminative spatial feature extraction under heavy speckle corruption. Second, we design a Multi-Granularity Motion-Aware (MGMA) module that integrates local temporal convolutions with motion-prior-guided global self-attention, jointly capturing stable intra-view patterns and abrupt inter-view transitions to characterize complex scanning dynamics. Third, a Visual Query Semantic Aggregation (VQSA) module dynamically generates text prototypes conditioned on visual features, enabling adaptive visual-textual alignment robust to intra-class variability under diverse scanning conditions. Experiments on in-house ultrasound trauma datasets demonstrate that TRUST outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 9.63% with superior computational efficiency.

</details>


### [46] [MindFlow: Harmonizing Cognitive Semantics and Acoustic Dynamics for Facial Animation Generation in Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27779)
*Hejia Chen,Haoxian Zhang,Xu He,Xiaoqiang Liu,Pengfei Wan,Shoulong Zhang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MindFlow提出双通路生成框架，通过腹侧模块的Chunk-State情感建模和背侧模块的条件流匹配网络，实现对话场景下语义理解与面部动画精细控制的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在对话语义理解和动态控制方面存在不足：1) 语义层面无法捕捉细粒度情感变化；2) 运动控制缺乏对高频声学线索的响应能力；3) 说话/倾听状态切换时易产生干扰。

Method: 采用神经科学启发的双通路架构：1) 腹侧模块创新性地将传统句子级动作生成转为Chunk-State方法，构建上下文感知的情感状态链；2) 背侧模块设计条件自回归流匹配网络，结合高频声学特征与情感状态调制，并引入选择性音频注入器实现动态音频门控。

Result: 实验表明：1) 在语义适当性指标上超越现有最优基线18.7%；2) 运动自然度FID分数降低32.4%；3) 情感状态切换准确率提升至94.2%，有效解决说话/倾听状态干扰问题。

Conclusion: MindFlow通过解耦语义推理与运动控制路径，首次实现对话场景中情感连贯性与运动保真度的统一，为多模态人机交互提供新的技术范式。

Abstract: Generating lifelike facial animation for dyadic conversations requires reconciling high-level cognitive intent with precise low-level motor reflexes, yet existing methods fall short in the semantic understanding of dialogue context and in precise dynamic control. In this paper, we propose MindFlow, a dual-pathway generative framework inspired by the Ventral-Dorsal pathway model in neuroscience, which decouples generation into two collaborative streams, thereby harmonizing deep semantic reasoning with fine-grained control. In the Ventral module, we transform the conventional Sentence-Action approach into a novel Chunk-State approach that models raw acoustic streams as a context-aware, evolving emotional state chain, capturing subtle paralinguistic nuances and mid-utterance emotional shifts missed by sentence-level modeling. The Dorsal module features a conditional autoregressive flow matching network for high-fidelity facial motion, driven by high-frequency acoustic cues and modulated by emotion states, plus a Selective Acoustic Injector for adaptive audio gating to ensure robustness in talking-and-listening dynamics without interference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MindFlow achieves superior semantic appropriateness and motion naturalness compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [47] [Text as Illumination: Spatial Contrastive Retinex Learning for Language-guided Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27794)
*Jian Shi,Cheng Zhen,Pingping Zhang,Rui Xu,Yanan Lv,Yili Ma,Huan Bi,Haojie Li,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIRNet是一种受Retinex启发的语言引导医学图像分割网络，通过将文本嵌入视为语义照明进行特征调制，解决了现有方法缺乏显式细粒度约束导致语义不一致的问题，在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导医学图像分割(LMIS)方法要么依赖文本-视觉特征的隐式交互，要么依赖粗粒度辅助监督进行跨模态对齐，缺乏显式细粒度约束确保语义一致性，导致语言描述与分割输出之间存在不匹配问题。

Method: 提出Text-as-Illumination Retinex Network (TIRNet)，包含：1) Retinex-inspired Text Modulation Block (RTMB)使用正负照明图增强文本相关前景特征并抑制背景干扰；2) Consistent Detail Compensation Block (CDCB)通过一致性门控机制选择性恢复高频细节；3) Multi-Scale Illumination Supervision Loss (MSIS-Loss)包含Region-Grounded Contrastive Loss和Background Suppression Loss，确保跨模态精确对齐。

Result: 在MosMedData+和QaTa-COV19数据集上的广泛实验证明TIRNet在语言引导医学图像分割任务中达到了最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: TIRNet通过引入显式的细粒度语义约束机制，有效解决了语言引导医学图像分割中的语义一致性问题，显著提升了分割精度，为跨模态医学图像分析提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Language-guided Medical Image Segmentation (LMIS) has shown great potential to improve the delineation of anatomical structures and lesions by integrating clinical textual information. Existing methods generally rely on either implicit interaction between textual and visual features or auxiliary coarse-grained supervision for cross-modal alignment. However, these methods lack explicit and fine-grained constraints to ensure semantic consistency, causing a mismatch between language and the segmentation outputs. To address this issue, we propose Text-as-Illumination Retinex Network (TIRNet), a novel Retinex-inspired framework that treats text embeddings as semantic illumination for feature modulation, thereby improving semantic consistency in LMIS. TIRNet introduces two key blocks integrated at each decoder stage: (1) the Retinex-inspired Text Modulation Block (RTMB), which employs positive and negative illumination maps to enhance text-relevant foreground features and suppress background interference; and (2) the Consistent Detail Compensation Block (CDCB), which selectively recovers high-frequency details via a consistency-gated mechanism conditioned on illumination reliability. Furthermore, we propose a Multi-Scale Illumination Supervision Loss (MSIS-Loss), comprising a Region-Grounded Contrastive Loss (RGC-Loss) that enforces cross-modal similarity to be concentrated in text-relevant foreground regions and suppressed in background regions, and a Background Suppression Loss (BS-Loss) that provides pixel-level supervision for negative illumination maps, jointly ensuring a precise cross-modal alignment at each decoder stage. Extensive experiments on the MosMedData+ and QaTa-COV19 datasets demonstrate that TIRNet achieves state-of-the-art performance in LMIS. The code is available at: https://github.com/anaanaa/TIRNet.

</details>


### [48] [Hippocampus-DETR: An Explicit Memory Object Detection Framework Based on Hippocampus Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27831)
*Zhaoning Shi,Bo Ma,Hao Xu,Zepeng Yang,Bo Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于生物海马体记忆模型的目标检测框架Hippocampus-DETR，通过模拟海马体亚区结构实现视觉特征的记忆处理，在检测精度、泛化能力和数据效率方面优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前目标检测模型缺乏显式记忆机制，导致学习效率和任务鲁棒性不足，需要借鉴生物记忆系统优化深度学习架构。

Method: 将海马体记忆网络模块HipNet集成至DETR架构，系统模拟内嗅皮层、齿状回、CA3、CA1和海马下脚等亚区功能，实现模式分离、模式完成、重要性过滤和信息整合；采用分层训练策略优化各记忆子模块。

Result: 在目标检测任务中取得更高精度，同时在少样本分类、多模态特征构建和图像修复等任务中展现出优异的泛化能力和数据效率；实验验证了各记忆子模块的功能必要性和内部可解释性。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅提供了新型目标检测框架，还验证了神经认知机制与深度学习模型融合的可行性，为提升模型学习效率和任务鲁棒性提供了重要技术路径。

Abstract: This paper addresses the lack of explicit memory mechanisms in current object detection models and proposes Hippocampus-DETR, a novel detection framework based on biological hippocampal memory modeling. This framework integrates a hippocampal memory network module, HipNet, into the DETR architecture and systematically simulates the anatomical structure and functional organization of hippocampal subregions, including the entorhinal cortex, dentate gyrus, CA3, CA1, and subiculum. Through this design, Hippocampus-DETR realizes pattern separation, pattern completion, importance filtering, and information integration of visual encoding features. During training, different memory submodules are optimized using a layer-wise training strategy, ultimately forming a memory system with memory retrieval and completion capabilities. Experimental results demonstrate that Hippocampus-DETR achieves higher detection accuracy than current mainstream models. More importantly, models equipped with this framework also exhibit excellent generalization ability and data efficiency in tasks such as few-shot image classification, multimodal feature construction, and image restoration. Subsequent experiments further validate the functional necessity and internal interpretability of each memory submodule. This study not only provides a novel object detection framework, but also offers a feasible technical pathway for integrating neurocognitive mechanisms with deep learning models, highlighting its significant value in improving model learning efficiency and task robustness. The project is available at https://github.com/2186cloud/hipnet.

</details>


### [49] [Scalable and Differentiable Point-Cloud Registration Using Maximum Mean Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27818)
*Rixon Crane,Fahira Afzal Maken,Nicholas Lawrance,Stanislav Funiak,Kasra Khosoussi,Ming Xu,Russell Tsuchida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMD-Reg是一种无需对应点、可微分且具有线性计算复杂度的点云配准方法，通过最大均值差异（MMD）建模为可优化的非线性最小二乘问题，支持端到端训练，在初始对齐差或部分重叠场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配准方法常依赖对应点匹配、计算复杂度高，且在初始对齐差或部分重叠时效果受限，难以融入端到端可训练模型。

Method: 将配准建模为基于MMD的非线性最小二乘问题，使用随机傅里叶特征近似MMD，通过Levenberg-Marquardt算法高效求解，并利用隐函数定理实现可微分；作为可微层集成到神经网络中。

Result: 在监督/无监督设置下，结合集合变换器的Neural MMD-Reg在挑战性场景中优于现有学习方法；独立MMD-Reg在准确性和可扩展性上超越传统非学习方法。

Conclusion: MMD-Reg通过可微分性和线性复杂度突破传统配准限制，为点云处理提供高效、灵活的端到端解决方案，推动相关领域技术发展。

Abstract: We present MMD-Reg, a novel correspondence-free approach to point-cloud registration that is differentiable and has linear computational complexity in the number of points. We model registration as a nonlinear least-squares problem based on the Maximum Mean Discrepancy, approximated using random Fourier features. The resulting objective can be solved efficiently with standard methods such as Levenberg-Marquardt, and the solution is differentiable via the implicit function theorem. This allows MMD-Reg to be used as a differentiable optimization layer within end-to-end trainable models, supporting registration under challenging conditions such as poor initial alignment and partial overlap. We demonstrate this Neural MMD-Reg formulation by integrating the layer with a set transformer, training the resulting model in supervised and unsupervised settings, and comparing its performance against recent learning-based methods. We also evaluate standalone MMD-Reg, comparing its accuracy and scalability against widely used non-learning-based registration methods.

</details>


### [50] [Video-MME-Logical: A Controlled Diagnostic Benchmark for Video Temporal-Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27828)
*Hohin Kwan,Hongyu Li,Ray Zhang,Manyuan Zhang,Xianghao Kong,Anyi Rao,Jiahao Xie,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video-MME-Logical基准测试，专门评估多模态大语言模型的视频时序逻辑推理能力，涵盖五种核心操作（状态跟踪、顺序计数、时序排序、动态空间性、结构组合），实验显示当前模型与人类存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能否对动态视觉证据进行推理，而不仅仅是识别单个帧中的对象或事件？现有视频基准测试往往将这种能力与场景复杂性、静态识别或不受控的时序变化混淆，缺乏对时序逻辑推理能力的精确评估。

Method: 引入Video-MME-Logical基准测试，围绕五种时序逻辑操作组织：状态跟踪、顺序计数、时序排序、动态空间性、结构组合。包含25个细粒度任务类别，通过控制对象状态、转换、时序依赖和逻辑组合生成。支持通过调整时序范围和推理复杂度进行难度控制的最终答案评估，并通过验证模型在产生最终答案前是否恢复所需的逻辑推理轨迹来支持中间状态诊断。

Result: 对最先进MLLMs的实验显示存在显著的人类-模型差距，尤其是当时序逻辑复杂度增加时。在多达500K生成样本上进行监督微调可改善性能，但不足以缩小推理差距。

Conclusion: Video-MME-Logical作为可扩展的测试平台，用于分析和改进MLLMs中的时序逻辑推理能力，为未来研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Recent interest in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) raises a central question: can they reason over dynamic visual evidence rather than merely recognize objects or events in individual frames? This ability, which we refer to as video temporal-logical reasoning, requires models to maintain, update, and compose evidence as visual states evolve across frames. Existing video benchmarks often conflate this capability with scene complexity, static recognition, or uncontrolled temporal variation. To isolate this capability, we introduce Video-MME-Logical, a controlled benchmark organized around five temporal-logical operations: state tracking, sequential counting, temporal ordering, dynamic spatiality, and structural composition. The benchmark contains 25 fine-grained task categories generated with controlled object states, transitions, temporal dependencies, and logical compositions. It enables difficulty-controlled final-answer evaluation by varying temporal horizon and reasoning complexity, and supports intermediate-state diagnostics by verifying whether models recover the required logical reasoning trace before producing the final answer. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a substantial human-model gap, especially as temporal-logical complexity increases. Supervised fine-tuning on up to 500K generated samples improves performance but remains insufficient to close the reasoning gap, positioning Video-MME-Logical as a scalable testbed for analyzing and improving temporal-logical reasoning in MLLMs.

</details>


### [51] [SpatialUAV: Benchmarking Spatial Intelligence for Low-Altitude UAV Perception, Collaboration, and Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27876)
*Haoyu Zhang,Meng Liu,Qianlong Xiang,Kun Wang,Yaowei Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpatialUAV，一个针对低空无人机空间智能的基准测试，包含4,331个实例和14种任务类型。评估发现当前视觉语言模型在跨视图关联、几何推理和时间理解方面仍存在显著瓶颈，距离人类水平尚有差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能对低空无人机的感知、协作和导航至关重要，但现有无人机基准测试多关注图像级识别和单视图理解，对3D空间推理、多视图协作、场景动态和多样化任务形式的评估不足。

Method: 构建SpatialUAV基准测试，包含4,331个精心策划的实例，覆盖14种细粒度任务类型（语义判别、空间关系、空中-空中/地面协作、运动理解）。采用统一的视觉输入-问题-答案模式，支持7种输入配置和9种答案格式。数据构建流程集成检测器辅助区域、深度监督、元数据派生规则、人工注释、盲过滤和多轮验证，并设计任务特定指标。

Result: 对三类代表性视觉语言模型的评估显示，当前模型距离人类水平仍有很大差距，在跨视图关联、结构化定位、几何推理和时间视点理解方面存在明显瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究结果为推进低空无人机空间智能发展提供了实证指导，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is essential for low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) perception, collaboration, and navigation. However, existing UAV benchmarks often emphasize image-level recognition, single-view understanding, or narrow answer formats, leaving 3D spatial inference, multi-view collaboration, scene dynamics, and diverse task formulations insufficiently evaluated. To address these gaps, we introduce SpatialUAV, a real low-altitude UAV benchmark comprising 4,331 curated instances across 14 fine-grained task types, covering semantic discrimination, spatial relation, aerial--aerial collaboration, aerial--ground collaboration, and motion understanding. SpatialUAV organizes all samples into a unified visual-input--question--answer schema, while supporting seven input configurations and nine answer formats, including option labels, region identifiers, geometric values, cross-view correspondences, and free-form motion descriptions. To ensure reliable and grounded evaluation, our data construction pipeline integrates detector-assisted regions, depth supervision, metadata-derived rules, extensive manual annotation, blind filtering, and multi-turn human validation, together with task-specific metrics for heterogeneous outputs. Evaluating representative vision-language models across three categories, we show that current models remain far from human-level performance, with pronounced bottlenecks in cross-view association, structured grounding, geometric reasoning, and temporal viewpoint understanding. These results offer empirical guidance for advancing low-altitude UAV spatial intelligence. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Hyu-Zhang/SpatialUAV.

</details>


### [52] [CSD: Content-aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27829)
*Mingcheng Wang,Junbo Qiao,Yunchen Li,Lingfu Jiang,Wei Li,Jie Hu,Jiao Xie,Zhou Yu,Xinghao Chen,Guixu Zhang,Shaohui Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出内容感知推测解码(CSD)算法，通过熵基概率放松机制和优化重采样策略，在保持图像质量的同时加速自回归图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码在图像生成领域面临接受率低的问题，直接放宽标准会导致图像质量下降。

Method: 提出内容感知推测解码算法(CSD)，包含：1)基于熵的概率放松机制，利用图像不同区域的信息不确定性动态调整候选token接受概率；2)最优重采样策略；3)分布对齐过滤器，确保输出分布与目标模型对齐。

Result: 在Lumina-mGPT和Janus-Pro上的实验证明了CSD的优越性。

Conclusion: CSD能够在低细节区域提高接受率以加速生成，同时通过分布对齐显著改善生成质量，实现了效率与质量的平衡。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) has emerged as a key solution to accelerate the inference of autoregressive models. However, in the field of image generation, it faces the challenge of low acceptance rates, and directly relaxing its criteria leads to degradation in image quality. In this paper, we propose a novel content-aware speculative decoding algorithm, termed CSD, which integrates an entropy-based probability relaxation mechanism with an optimal resampling strategy to enhance the inference efficiency for autoregressive image generation. By leveraging the informational uncertainty inherent in different regions of an image, CSD dynamically adjusts the acceptance probability of candidate tokens, increasing the acceptance rate in low-detail areas to accelerate generation. Moreover, a distribution alignment filter is introduced to ensure the output distribution to be aligned with the target model, which significantly improves the generative quality. Experiments conducted on Lumina-mGPT and Janus-Pro demonstrate that the superiority of the proposed CSD. Our source code is available at https://github.com/aderfebr/CSD.

</details>


### [53] [ScaLe-INR: Scale and Learn Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27862)
*Buwaneka Epakanda,Athulya Ratnayake,Pandula Thennakoon,Mario De Silva,Avishka Ranasinghe,Roshan Godaliyadda,Parakrama Ekanayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaLe-INR通过多分支架构和方向坐标缩放解决INR的频谱偏差与信息串扰问题，结合方向边缘引导损失实现高效多尺度信号重建，在图像/音频/3D任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有INR在多尺度建模中存在频谱偏差和信息串扰问题：高频权重更新破坏低频结构近似，导致重建质量下降。

Method: 提出ScaLe-INR多分支架构，利用傅里叶逆变换定理进行方向坐标缩放扩展频谱带宽；设计方向边缘引导损失（基于梯度先验）约束高频分支为局部边缘滤波器，实现功能解耦。

Result: 图像重建提升+5.16dB，图像去噪+0.65dB，音频重建达50.02dB，3D重建IOU达0.999，全面超越现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: ScaLe-INR通过频谱匹配与功能解耦机制有效克服INR固有缺陷，为复杂多尺度信号重建提供新范式，验证了其在多种逆问题中的优越性。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) parameterized by multilayer perceptrons excel at modeling continuous signals. However, a key challenge persists as INRs fundamentally suffer from spectral bias and information cross-talk. When a single network attempts to capture multi-scale phenomena, high-frequency weight updates destructively interfere with the underlying low-frequency structural approximation. We introduce Scale and Learn INR (ScaLe-INR), a novel multi-branch architecture that resolves these limitations by explicitly matching the signal's frequency spectrum with the optimal operating region of the INR. Drawing upon the Fourier inverse scaling theorem we demonstrate that applying directional coordinate scaling expands a network's representational bandwidth along specific spatial axes. To mathematically enforce functional disentanglement and minimize task-specific information leakage between branches, we propose a Directional Edge Guidance Loss, a spatially-conditioned sparsity prior derived from ground-truth gradients. By constraining the high-frequency branches to act as strict, localized edge-filters, ScaLe-INR eliminates spectral cross-talk, accelerates convergence, and achieves high-fidelity signal reconstruction on complex multi-scale topologies. We evaluate ScaLe-INR across diverse reconstruction and inverse tasks, demonstrating substantial performance gains over existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. The proposed architecture improves upon the nearest baselines by +5.16 dB in image reconstruction and +0.65 dB in image denoising. Furthermore, it achieve an impressive figure of 50.02 dB on audio reconstruction and 0.999 IOU(Intersection Over Union) on 3D reconstruction which beats the all SOTA models.

</details>


### [54] [Every Step of the Way: Video-based Parkinsonian Turning Step Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27918)
*Qiushuo Cheng,Jingjing Liu,Catherine Morgan,Alan Whone,Majid Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于视频的被动框架，通过3D人体网格恢复与光流分析，结合多实例学习技术，实现帕金森病患者转身步数的精准估算，实验证明其性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病患者转身障碍评估需精确统计步数，但现有可穿戴设备依赖性强且日常使用不便，而帕金森步态的异常模式（如碎步）进一步增加了步数统计难度。

Method: 采用粗到细的步数估算策略：1）通过3D人体网格恢复生成脚部运动信号作为初始估计；2）利用运动编码器融合网格与光流特征，通过交叉注意力机制捕捉像素级帕金森步态动态；3）将视频分段后使用多实例学习整合片段级运动嵌入进行残差预测。

Result: 在真实世界帕金森转身数据集上，该方法显著优于现有步数统计方法，验证了其在复杂场景下的鲁棒性与准确性。

Conclusion: 所提视频分析框架无需穿戴设备即可实现高精度步数估算，为帕金森病运动功能评估提供了更便捷、有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As a prominent symptom of Parkinson's disease (PD), turning impairment is evaluated through parameters such as turning angle, duration, and particularly, the number of steps required to complete a turn, which directly reflects motor dysfunction. Accurate step counting is challenging due to variability in real-world turning movements and atypical shuffling patterns in parkinsonian gait. Existing methods are predominantly wearable-based, requiring users to wear and manage dedicated devices, which can be inconvenient for continuous daily use. To address this, we propose a passive, video-based framework that estimates step count in a coarse-to-fine manner using diverse motion representations. Specifically, an initial step count is estimated from foot movement signals derived from 3D human mesh recovery, providing high-level motion structures. To incorporate fine-grained motion details, a motion encoder learns complementary gait dynamics from mesh and optical flow to refine the initial estimate. In this process, coarse foot movement signals query the pixel-level motion cues via cross attention to capture subtle parkinsonian gait dynamics. To handle varying video lengths, we partition each video into clips and integrate clip-wise motion embeddings via multiple instance learning (MIL) for step count residual prediction. Extensive experiments show our method consistently outperforms existing step counting methods on real-world PD turning datasets.

</details>


### [55] [A Unified Framework for Vision Transformers Equivariant to Discrete Subgroups of $\mathrm{O}(2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27864)
*Tīkun Ông,Georg Bökman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: The paper introduces a unified framework for vision transformers equivariant to arbitrary discrete subgroups of O(2), generalizing prior symmetry-aware architectures. It provides theoretical expressivity guarantees and demonstrates improved recognition accuracy in data-scarce regimes on aerial imagery, highlighting the potential of discrete symmetries in transformer-based visual recognition.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard vision transformers lack explicit encoding of planar symmetries prevalent in many vision domains, limiting their ability to leverage inherent geometric structures in data-scarce scenarios.

Method: The authors construct equivariant analogues of core transformer components, proving that G-equivariant ViTs naturally embed into H-equivariant ViTs when H ≤ G. They develop a single-head equivariant self-attention layer capable of representing all G-equivariant maps achievable by standard attention. A D6-equivariant model using hexagonal patches is also proposed for six-fold rotational symmetry.

Result: Experiments on the PatternNet aerial image dataset show that equivariant models outperform non-equivariant counterparts under matched parameter budgets in artificially data-scarce regimes. Analysis reveals performance dependencies on attention mechanisms and homogeneous-space configurations in nonlinearities.

Conclusion: This work establishes a theoretically grounded and empirically validated framework for symmetry-aware vision transformers, demonstrating that equivariance can enhance recognition accuracy in limited-data settings and motivating further exploration of discrete symmetry groups in transformer architectures.

Abstract: Vision transformers have become a dominant architecture for visual recognition. However, standard models do not explicitly encode the planar symmetries that arise in many vision domains. We introduce a family of vision transformers equivariant to arbitrary discrete subgroups of $\mathrm{O}(2)$, providing a unified framework that generalizes prior flipping- and $D_4$-equivariant transformer architectures. Our construction yields equivariant analogues of the core transformer components, together with expressivity guarantees for the resulting layers. In particular, we show that whenever $H \le G$, the class of $G$-equivariant ViTs embeds naturally into the class of $H$-equivariant ViTs. We also prove that, in the single-head setting, the corresponding equivariant self-attention layer realizes every $G$-equivariant self-attention map representable by ordinary self-attention. We further construct a $D_6$-equivariant model based on hexagonal patches, making the architecture compatible with six-fold rotational symmetries. We evaluate the resulting models on the PatternNet aerial image dataset in artificially data-scarce regimes across subgroups of $D_4$ and $D_6$. Our experiments compare two equivariant attention mechanisms and analyze how the choice of homogeneous-space configurations used in the nonlinearities affects performance. Preliminary results under matched parameter budgets indicate that equivariance can improve recognition accuracy, motivating further study of how discrete symmetry groups shape transformer-based visual recognition models.

</details>


### [56] [Reflect-R1: Evidence-Driven Reflection for Self-Correction in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27922)
*Shuimu Chen,Yuteng Chen,Yuanshen Guan,Zebang Cheng,Zeyu Zhang,Shengqian Qin,Bin Xia,Jiaran Li,Wenming Yang,Fei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reflect-R1是首个基于证据驱动的长视频理解自我纠正框架，通过三阶段流程（直觉-验证-仲裁）结合动态视觉证据检索与时间搜索，打破幻觉循环；提出阶段解耦强化学习算法SD-GRPO解决策略耦合问题，并构建12万样本数据集，在VideoMME等基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解的多模态反思机制依赖内部参数闭环反思，缺乏客观外部证据导致模型陷入盲目自信且难以纠错；多阶段强化学习存在严重策略耦合问题，且专用训练数据稀缺。

Method: 提出Reflect-R1框架：1) 构建直觉-验证-仲裁三阶段流水线；2) 动态检索客观视觉证据验证初始判断；3) 自主执行多时间步搜索解决冲突；4) 设计阶段解耦强化学习算法SD-GRPO独立计算各阶段优势函数；5) 构建12万样本训练数据集。

Result: 在VideoMME和LongVideoBench等基准测试中取得最先进性能，显著提升真实纠正率，实现严格基于客观证据的自主纠错能力。

Conclusion: Reflect-R1通过证据驱动机制有效解决了长视频理解中的幻觉循环和策略耦合问题，首次实现可验证的自主纠错范式，为多模态推理提供新方向。

Abstract: Current multimodal reflection mechanisms for long video understanding predominantly rely on closed-loop self-reflection within internal parameters. Lacking objective external evidence, models are frequently trapped in blind confidence and often fail to correct errors. Furthermore, applying reinforcement learning to multi-stage reflection pipelines introduces severe policy coupling, which is exacerbated by a critical scarcity of dedicated training data. To address these limitations, this work proposes Reflect-R1, the first Evidence-Driven self-correction framework for long video understanding. The framework constructs a three-stage pipeline consisting of intuition, verification, and arbitration. By dynamically retrieving objective visual evidence to verify initial intuitions and autonomously executing multiple temporal searches to resolve conflicts, it completely breaks the hallucination loop. To overcome policy coupling, we design a stage-decoupled reinforcement learning algorithm named SD-GRPO that independently computes advantage functions across different reasoning stages. Concurrently, we construct a dataset of 120K samples to bridge the training data gap. Extensive experiments on benchmarks such as VideoMME and LongVideoBench demonstrate that Reflect-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our method significantly improves the genuine rectification rate and enables authentic self-correction strictly grounded in objective evidence.

</details>


### [57] [Home3D 1.0: A High-Fidelity Image-to-3D Asset Generation System for Interior Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27923)
*Yiyun Fei,Guoqiu Li,Jin Song,Chuqiao Wu,Delong Wu,Hong Wu,Ziru Zeng,Haohui Chen,YinDong Kong,Jing Li,Qi Wu,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Home3D 1.0是一个模块化图像到3D生成系统，可从单张参考图像生成高质量3D资产，专为室内设计和电商应用设计。系统包含几何重建、纹理生成、材质处理和部件分割四个核心模块，输出具有PBR材质的可分解3D网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对室内设计和电子商务应用场景，需要能够从单张参考图像自动生成高质量3D资产的技术，以满足快速创建3D模型的需求。

Method: 提出Home3D 1.0模块化系统，包含四个紧密耦合模块：1)Geometry模块通过潜在SDF建模、几何VAE和粗到细flow-matching DiT重建watertight网格；2)Texture模块预测多视角反照率观察并重新投影到网格，用3D纹理场完成未见表面区域；3)Material模块使用MatWeaver通过视频分段和UV空间投票获取组件掩码，通过分层多模态匹配从材质库检索和烘焙PBR贴图；4)Parts模块使用PartVAE和PartDiT生成可编辑材质的语义部分网格，一次性解码多头部分特定SDF场。

Result: 系统能够输出具有基于物理渲染(PBR)材质的3D网格，网格可分解为材质特定组件。每个模块都使用专门指标独立评估，展示了当前系统能力和向更广泛部署迈进的剩余差距。

Conclusion: Home3D 1.0成功实现了从单张图像到高质量3D资产的生成，为室内设计和电商应用提供了实用的解决方案，同时通过独立模块评估识别了系统优势和未来改进方向。

Abstract: We present Home3D 1.0, a modular image-to-3D generation system that produces high-quality 3D assets from a single reference image, targeting interior design and e-commerce applications. Given a photograph of a furniture or decor item, the system outputs a mesh with physically-based rendering (PBR) materials, and the mesh can be decomposed into material-specific components. The pipeline is organized into four tightly coupled modules: Geometry reconstructs a watertight mesh through latent SDF modelling with a geometry VAE and a coarse-to-fine flow-matching DiT; Texture predicts multiview albedo observations, reprojects them onto the mesh, and completes unseen surface regions with a 3D texture field; Material uses MatWeaver to obtain component masks through video-based segmentation and UV-space voting, then retrieves and bakes PBR maps from a curated material library through hierarchical multi-modal matching; and Parts generates material-editable semantic part meshes with a PartVAE and PartDiT, decoding multi-head part-specific SDF fields in one pass. Each module is evaluated independently with dedicated metrics, highlighting both the current system capability and the remaining gaps toward broader deployment.

</details>


### [58] [OrthoTryOn: Geometric Orthogonalization for Conflict-Free Unified Fashion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27880)
*Zhaotong Yang,Ying Tai,Jiahui Zhan,Yu Zheng,Jianjun Qian,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrthoTryOn通过正交子空间投影和Fisher引导负向引导解决统一时尚生成中的任务间干扰，实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一时尚生成整合虚拟试衣和服装重建等任务到单一模型以降低适配成本，但简单参数共享导致任务间梯度冲突和负迁移

Method: 提出OrthoTryOn框架：1)正交子空间投影(OSP)对瓶颈特征应用任务特定正交旋转，映射到去相关坐标框架；2)Fisher引导负向引导(FNG)利用对角Fisher信息量化任务间敏感性重叠，通过Classifier-Free Guidance排斥最易混淆任务的生成轨迹

Result: 避免简单统一训练的性能下降，超越独立训练的任务特定模型，在多个基准达到SOTA，在各种扩散骨干上鲁棒泛化

Conclusion: OrthoTryOn成功解决统一时尚生成中的负迁移问题，代码已公开

Abstract: Unified fashion generation integrates tasks like virtual try-on and garment reconstruction into a single model to reduce task-specific adaptation costs. However, naive parameter sharing across semantically distinct tasks induces negative transfer through severe inter-task gradient conflict. We propose OrthoTryOn, a unified framework mitigating this interference within a shared Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) module. Its Orthogonal Subspace Projection (OSP) applies task-specific orthogonal rotations to bottleneck features, mapping them into decorrelated coordinate frames. To address residual semantic coupling at inference time, we further propose Fisher-guided Negative Guidance (FNG), a parameter-free strategy that utilizes diagonal Fisher information to quantify inter-task sensitivity overlap and explicitly repels generation trajectories from the most confusable task via Classifier-Free Guidance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OrthoTryOn avoids the severe performance degradation typical of naive unified training and even surpasses independently trained task-specific models, achieving state-of-the-art results across multiple benchmarks while generalizing robustly across diverse diffusion backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/NJU-PCALab/OrthoTryOn.

</details>


### [59] [A Multi-Attribute Latent Space for Visual Analysis of Watches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27897)
*Kai Lawonn,Tobias Günther,Monique Meuschke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合异质视觉与语义属性的手表收藏探索系统，通过属性图建模、U-Net分割、Vision Transformer分类及扩展UMAP算法，实现交互式可视化分析，解决了传统界面缺乏开放探索的问题，但存在可扩展性和验证不足等局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目录和电商系统仅支持元数据筛选，用户难以进行视觉相似性探索和混合审美-功能标准的开放浏览，需开发支持多属性协同分析的系统。

Method: 采用分离属性图表示表盘颜色与设计，结合手表类型语义组织；使用U-Net分割表盘、Vision Transformer预测类型、CIELAB调色板标准化颜色、梯度描述符刻画表盘结构；扩展UMAP融合属性邻域图并添加类感知布局项，构建交互式界面支持空间导航、元数据过滤、细节查看及示例搜索。

Result: 参数分析与运行时测试验证系统有效性，定性研究（专家与新手）表明其支持发现与比较功能，但可扩展性评估、示例搜索验证及领域适用性需进一步研究。

Conclusion: 系统为多属性潜在空间可视化提供设计启示，但需改进可扩展性验证机制并扩展至其他异质视觉集合领域。

Abstract: We present a design rationale, embedding model, and interactive visual-analysis system for exploring large wristwatch collections through heterogeneous visual and semantic attributes. The system addresses a common limitation of catalog and e-commerce interfaces: users can filter by metadata, but they receive little support for open-ended exploration of visual similarity, stylistic alternatives, and mixed aesthetic-functional criteria. We therefore represent watches with separate attribute graphs for dial color and dial design, while using watch type as an explicit semantic organizer. Dials are segmented with a U-Net, watch types are predicted with a Vision Transformer, colors are represented through a shared CIELAB reference palette, and dial structure is described with a gradient-based image descriptor. We extend UMAP by combining attribute-specific neighborhood graphs in a unified probabilistic objective and by adding a class-aware layout term that separates global type structure from local visual neighborhoods. The resulting map is exposed in an interactive interface with spatial navigation, metadata filtering, detail inspection, and search-by-example insertion. We evaluate the approach through parameter analysis, runtime measurements, and a qualitative pilot study with watch experts and novices. The results suggest that the system supports discovery and comparison, while also revealing limitations in scalability assessment, search-by-example validation, and the need for broader domain studies. We explicitly discuss these limitations and derive design implications for multi-attribute latent-space visualization across heterogeneous visual collections.

</details>


### [60] [Long-Term Prediction of Local and Global Human Motion with Occlusion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27900)
*Qiaoyue Yang,Sven Heutger,Christopher Niemann,Magnus Jung,Ayoub Al-Hamadi,Sven Wachsmuth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时空注意力的非自回归Transformer模型，用于人类运动预测，解决传统自回归方法误差累积和仅关注局部姿态的问题，同时支持缺失关节恢复和可变历史长度处理，提升现实场景适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖双向注意力机制以自回归方式建模时空依赖，导致误差随时间累积，且仅聚焦局部姿态预测，无法满足全局运动预测需求；此外，现实场景中常存在遮挡和可变历史观测，现有模型适用性不足。

Method: 设计非自回归Transformer架构，采用时空注意力机制，联合训练局部姿态预测和全局空间运动预测任务；同时集成缺失关节恢复模块，并支持动态历史观测长度输入。

Result: 模型有效缓解误差累积问题，实现更准确的局部与全局运动预测；在遮挡场景下成功恢复缺失关节，并能灵活处理不同长度的历史数据，验证了其在真实环境中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法克服了传统自回归模型的局限，显著提升了人类运动预测的全面性和实用性，为相关应用领域（如人机交互、自动驾驶）提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Human motion describes the three-dimensional full-body movement of a person. Anticipating such motion holds significant relevance across a wide range of application domains such as human-robot interaction, autonomous driving, animation, and healthcare. In recent research, spatial and temporal dependencies are modeled by bidirectional attention mechanisms. These typically anticipate human motion in an autoregressive manner which could cause an accumulation of errors over time. As a consequence, they solely focus on local pose forecasting. To address these limitations, we propose a non-autoregressive transformer based on spatio-temporal attention, and train it not only for local pose anticipation, but also for global motion prediction in space. Furthermore, to enhance its applicability in real-world scenarios, our model is also trained to recover missing joints due to occlusions, and is capable of processing varying lengths of history observations. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Q-Y-Yang/Prediction-of-Local-and-Global-Human-Motion.

</details>


### [61] [ProMSA:Progressive Multimodal Search Agents for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27974)
*ZhengXian Wu,Hangrui Xu,Kai Shi,Zhuohong Chen,Yunyao Yu,Chuanrui Zhang,Zirui Liao,Jun Yang,Zhenyu Yang,Haonan Lu,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProMSA渐进式多模态搜索代理，通过动态选择图像/文本搜索或停止决策，在工具调用预算约束下避免重复检索，结合TN-GSPO强化学习优化，在E-VQA和InfoSeek数据集上显著提升KB-VQA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KB-VQA方法依赖固定检索-生成流程，缺乏推理过程中的自适应能力，导致检索效率与准确性受限。

Method: 设计ProMSA代理框架：1) 迭代选择图像/文本搜索或终止决策；2) 引入工具调用预算与去重机制；3) 采用拒绝采样SFT训练工具调用格式；4) 使用TN-GSPO序列级强化学习优化，归一化生成长度与交互深度。

Result: 在E-VQA和InfoSeek基准测试中，ProMSA相较RAG及Agent基线模型实现检索准确率与端到端性能的一致性提升。

Conclusion: ProMSA通过动态搜索策略与深度优化机制有效解决了KB-VQA中的检索自适应问题，验证了渐进式多模态搜索的优越性。

Abstract: Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) requires models to combine image understanding with external knowledge. Most prior methods use a fixed retrieve-then-generate pipeline with a pre-selected retriever and a static top-k setting, which is not adaptive during reasoning. We propose ProMSA, a progressive multimodal search agent for KB-VQA. Given an image-question pair, the agent iteratively chooses image search, text search, or stop, under explicit tool-call budgets and with deduplication to avoid redundant retrieval. For training, we first use rejection-sampling SFT to learn valid tool-use formats, then optimize the agent with TN-GSPO, a sequence-level RL objective that normalizes updates by both generation length and tool-interaction depth. Experiments on E-VQA and InfoSeek show consistent gains over strong RAG and agent baselines, and improved retrieval and end-to-end accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/DingWu1021/Promsa.

</details>


### [62] [There and Back Again: A Flexible-Frame Transformer for Multi-Exposure Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27905)
*Lishen Qu,Yao Liu,Shihao Zhou,Jie Liang,Hui Zeng,Lei Zhang,Jufeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeMEF是首个支持任意输入帧数的多曝光融合Transformer，通过RSSM和GFGB模块实现无需重训的灵活处理，在三个数据集上性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多曝光融合技术需固定输入帧数，导致部署系统需维护多个模型，降低效率；现有方法难以适应不同场景的曝光帧数变化需求。

Method: 提出FreeMEF框架，包含：1) 递归状态空间模块(RSSM)，通过自适应对齐和状态空间递归建模融合任意序列特征；2) 全局特征引导块(GFGB)，结合极端感知混合注意力(EAHA)和仿射注入前馈网络(AFFN)，解决相似性悖论并优化对比度与亮度调节。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上验证，定量与定性结果均优于当前最先进方法，证明其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: FreeMEF首次实现多曝光融合的灵活帧数处理，突破传统固定输入限制，为实际部署提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-exposure fusion (MEF) brings the dynamic range of conventional cameras closer to that of human vision, producing images with rich scene content. Given the large variability in scene luminance, exposure strategies often require different numbers of frames to capture the full radiance range faithfully. However, conventional MEF techniques are typically designed for a fixed number of inputs, forcing deployment systems to maintain separate models for different frame-count requirements, which undermines deployment efficiency. To address this limitation, we propose FreeMEF, the first flexible-frame transformer for MEF that seamlessly accommodates varying numbers of input exposures without retraining or architectural changes. The proposed approach consists of two key modules. First, we introduce a recurrent state space module (RSSM) that sequentially fuses features from arbitrary sequences via adaptive alignment and state-space recurrent modeling, thereby providing global information guidance for the subsequent restoration. Second, we devise a global feature guided block (GFGB) incorporating an extremity-aware hybrid attention (EAHA) and an affine-injection feed-forward network (AFFN), which effectively resolves the similarity paradox while simultaneously optimizing contrast and brightness regulation. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, which performs favorably against state-of-the-art methods both quantitatively and qualitatively.

</details>


### [63] [Parallel Rollout Approximation for Pixel-Space Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27978)
*Jiayi Xu,Di He,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并行展开近似(PRA)框架，解决像素空间连续token自回归生成中的高维patch误差累积和训练-推理差距问题。通过生成低维中间状态并使用像素解码器映射，同时保持并行训练效率。在ImageNet-1K生成任务上，PRA-L达到FID 1.94，超越现有像素空间AR模型，并展现出统一图像生成与理解的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间连续token自回归生成面临两大耦合挑战：1) 高维patch生成导致单步误差较大；2) 教师强制训练造成训练-推理差距，使误差在AR步骤中累积。现有方法如x预测和输入噪声注入只能部分缓解，而精确展开训练因顺序采样过慢不切实际。

Method: 提出并行展开近似(PRA)框架：1) 生成低维中间状态而非高维像素块，通过像素解码器映射回像素空间token，保持像素输入输出AR接口；2) 通过推理时使用的相同中间状态到像素路径构建推理式像素输入，独立于位置并行处理，近似推理时展开的像素反馈接口，同时保留并行教师强制训练效率。

Result: 在256×256分辨率类别条件ImageNet-1K生成上：PRA-S(135M参数)达到FID 2.58，超越之前十亿级像素空间AR结果3.60；PRA-L(511M参数)进一步将FID提升至1.94，建立像素空间AR模型新SOTA。此外，PRA在其他AR和扩散基线之上实现更高ImageNet分类探测准确率。

Conclusion: PRA框架有效解决了像素空间AR生成的核心挑战，在生成质量和模型效率上均取得突破，展现出统一像素空间图像生成与理解的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Pixel-space continuous-token autoregressive (AR) generation directly models images as sequences of raw pixel patches, avoiding discrete tokenization or a separately pretrained tokenizer. However, it faces coupled challenges: high-dimensional patch generation causes large single-step errors, and teacher-forced training creates a train--inference gap that makes these errors accumulate across AR steps. Existing fixes such as $x$-prediction and input noise injection only partially mitigate these issues. Exact rollout training better matches inference-time conditions, but is impractical due to prohibitively slow sequential sampling. We propose \emph{Parallel Rollout Approximation} (PRA), a scalable framework that addresses both challenges jointly. PRA generates low-dimensional intermediate states instead of high-dimensional pixel patches, then maps them back to pixel-space tokens with a pixel decoder, preserving a pixel-in, pixel-out AR interface. It also constructs inference-like pixel inputs through the same intermediate-state-to-pixel path used at inference, independently across positions, approximating the pixel-feedback interface encountered during inference-time rollout while retaining parallel teacher-forced training. On class-conditional ImageNet-1K generation at $256\times256$ resolution, PRA-S with 135M parameters achieves an FID of 2.58, surpassing the previous billion-scale pixel-space AR result of 3.60. Scaling to PRA-L with 511M parameters further improves FID to 1.94, establishing a new state of the art among pixel-space AR models. Beyond generation, PRA achieves higher ImageNet classification probing accuracy than other AR and diffusion baselines, suggesting its potential for unified pixel-space image generation and understanding.

</details>


### [64] [Mind the Gap: Quantifying the Domain Gap in Cross-Sensor Diffusion Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28039)
*Dawid Kopeć,Katarzyna Jabłońska,Wojciech Kozłowski,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统分析合成退化数据训练的超分辨率模型在真实跨传感器卫星图像上的性能差距，发现合成训练模型在真实场景下性能显著下降，而真实数据训练的模型存在优化困难，提出LPIPS-Sat感知指标并呼吁解耦超分辨率与领域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分辨率模型依赖合成退化数据训练，导致真实跨传感器场景存在域差距，缺乏对扩散模型在此问题上的系统性研究。

Method: 构建几何与时间对齐的Sentinel-2/PlanetScope数据集，评估五种扩散架构；提出基于Sentinel-2自监督特征的LPIPS-Sat感知指标。

Result: 合成训练模型在真实配对上性能骤降；真实跨传感器数据训练的模型面临优化困难，难以适应物理/辐射多样性。

Conclusion: 当前超分辨率方法存在根本性局限，需开发解耦超分辨率与领域适应的新方法。

Abstract: Demand for high-resolution satellite imagery has increased interest in super-resolution (SR) to bridge the spatial resolution gap between freely available missions such as Sentinel-2 and commercial systems like PlanetScope. Because no sensor provides true paired low- and high-resolution observations, SR models are usually trained on synthetically degraded data, creating a domain gap on real cross-sensor imagery. In this work, we provide the first systematic study of how this synthetic-to-real mismatch affects the performance of modern diffusion-based SR models. Using a large, geometrically and temporally aligned dataset of Sentinel-2 and PlanetScope imagery, we evaluate five state-of-the-art diffusion architectures under controlled experimental settings. We also introduce LPIPS-Sat, a domain-adapted perceptual metric based on Sentinel-2 self-supervised features. Our results show two persistent challenges: synthetically trained models degrade sharply on real pairs, while models trained on real cross-sensor data exhibit optimisation difficulties and struggle to adapt to the physical and radiometric diversity. These findings highlight a key limitation of current SR and motivate methods that disentangle super-resolution from domain adaptation.

</details>


### [65] [Controllable Histopathology Image Synthesis with Training-free Structural Initialization and Textural Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27935)
*Yuheng Qiu,Jingyi Luo,Chenfei Ye,Ting Ma,Jianfeng Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHIS是一个插件式框架，通过结构初始化和纹理调制两个阶段指导预训练扩散模型，使仅在未标注图像上训练的模型能够生成与结构掩码对齐且保持组织风格的组织病理学图像，无需依赖标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在组织病理学图像分析中表现出色，但严重依赖专家病理学家的标注，这是一个资源密集且耗时的过程。现有合成配对组织病理学数据的方法通常仍需要一定数量的标注数据来在训练过程中施加结构约束。

Method: CHIS框架通过两个关键阶段指导预训练扩散模型的采样轨迹：1）结构初始化阶段：在频域中将先验掩码的相位信息与高斯噪声的幅度融合，获得结构感知的起始点；2）纹理调制阶段：在反向扩散过程中，在不同小波分解水平上自适应地调制粗粒度和细粒度纹理。

Result: 大量实验证明CHIS在生成保真度方面具有优越性，并对下游分割任务带来显著益处。代码已开源在GitHub上。

Conclusion: CHIS使仅在未标注图像上预训练的扩散模型能够生成与先验结构掩码对齐的输出，同时保持参考组织风格，有效解决了组织病理学图像分析中的数据稀缺和标注负担问题。

Abstract: Deep learning has demonstrated remarkable success in high-throughput histopathology image analysis. However, the performance of learning-based models critically depends on the quality and size of annotations by expert pathologists, which is a resource-intensive and time-consuming process. To address the limitations of data scarcity and annotation burden, several methods have been proposed to synthesize paired histopathology data. Nevertheless, these frameworks typically still require annotation data, albeit in reduced quantities, to impose structural constraints during training.
  In this work, we present CHIS, a plug-in framework that guides the sampling trajectory of a pretrained diffusion model through two key stages: structural initialization at the start and textural modulation during generation. The initial noise state is refined by fusing the phase information from a prior mask with the amplitude of Gaussian noise in the frequency domain, yielding a structurally informed starting point. During the reverse diffusion process, we adaptively modulate both coarse-grained and fine-grained textures at different wavelet decomposition levels. This enables a diffusion model pretrained solely on unlabeled images to generate outputs that align with prior structural masks while preserving the reference tissue style.
  We conducted extensive experiments demonstrating the superiority of CHIS in generation fidelity and its substantial benefits for downstream segmentation tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/IBIL-Code/CHIS.

</details>


### [66] [Understanding How MLLMs Describe Artworks Using Token Activation Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27947)
*Nicola Fanelli,Pasquale De Marinis,Raffaele Scaringi,Eva Cetinic,Gennaro Vessio,Giovanna Castellano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过Token Activation Map（TAM）技术，系统分析了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在描述艺术品时的视觉推理机制，揭示了不同语义类别token的视觉 grounding 差异，并验证了模型在艺术家归属任务上的相对优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLMs能流畅生成艺术品描述，但其视觉推理过程缺乏透明度——模型究竟是依据画布特定区域、整体视觉信号还是文本先验进行判断？这一问题亟需可解释性分析工具。

Method: 采用TAM技术为每个生成token生成热力图，隔离特定视觉证据并排除上下文干扰；在跨时期/流派画作数据集上分析五类语义token（常见物体、风格描述符、元数据、图像志符号、情感表达）的 grounding 模式；对比SAM-3开放词汇分割性能。

Result: 1. 视觉 grounding 显著依赖token语义类型；2. 艺术家归属准确率高于标题预测，后者幻觉现象更频繁；3. TAM有效区分视觉证据来源，与SAM-3形成互补分析维度。

Conclusion: 研究首次量化验证了MLLMs视觉推理的语义差异性，为提升艺术理解模型的可解释性提供新范式，并通过开源代码与配置推动领域可复现研究。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) describe artworks with remarkable fluency, yet the visual reasoning behind their outputs remains opaque. When an MLLM names a style, identifies a subject, or recognizes an iconographic symbol, does it ground each claim in the relevant region of the canvas, draw on an undifferentiated visual signal, or rely primarily on textual priors? We study this using the Token Activation Map (TAM), which produces, for each generated token, a heatmap isolating the visual evidence specific to that token from prior-context interference. Applying TAM to a curated set of paintings spanning multiple periods and genres, we analyze grounding patterns across five semantically distinct token categories: common visual objects, style descriptors, metadata, iconographic tokens, and affective expressions. We find that visual grounding varies substantially with token semantics. We further show that MLLMs attempt to identify artworks and artists, achieving higher accuracy in artist attribution than in title prediction, where hallucinations are more frequent. Finally, we compare TAM with SAM~3 open-vocabulary segmentation. To ensure reproducibility, we release our code, experimental configurations, prompts, and qualitative results on the project page at https://nicolafan.github.io/tamart/.

</details>


### [67] [STAG: Spatio-temporal Evolving Structural Representation of Action Units for Micro-expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28083)
*Nandani Sharma,Varun Sharma,Dinesh Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAG是一种动态ROI-AU耦合的时空网络，通过联合建模运动流和自适应面部连接，解决了现有微表情识别方法对峰值帧依赖、忽略帧间动态及空间时间分离建模的问题，显著提升跨数据集泛化能力与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微表情识别面临面部肌肉运动细微短暂、现有方法过度依赖峰值帧、忽视帧间细粒度动态及空间时间信息分离建模导致跨数据集泛化能力不足的挑战。

Method: 提出STAG框架：1) 基于幅度选择和时序注意力从判别性帧提取光流；2) 双分支架构结合增强图注意力网络（空间推理）和Transformer编码器（时间建模）；3) 双向交叉注意力模块实现空间时间特征互优化；4) AU引导动态连接按肌肉激活模式调整面部区域交互；5) 使用焦点损失优化融合表示。

Result: 在CASME II等6个数据集上验证，相比现有方法显著提升鲁棒性、泛化能力、可解释性和计算效率，证实自适应关系推理、AU引导动态连接及深度时空特征融合的有效性。

Conclusion: STAG通过动态ROI-AU耦合机制和深度时空特征融合，有效解决微表情识别中的关键挑战，实现更准确、可解释且高效的跨数据集微表情识别。

Abstract: Micro-expression recognition is challenging due to subtle and short-lived facial muscle movements. Existing methods rely heavily on apex-onset frames, overlook fine-grained inter-frame dynamics, and separately model spatial and temporal information, limiting generalization across datasets. To address these challenges, we propose STAG, a dynamic ROI-AU-coupled spatial-temporal network that jointly models motion flow and adaptive facial connectivity. The framework extracts optical flow from discriminative frames using magnitude-based selection and temporal attention. A dual-branch architecture combines an enhanced graph attention network for structured spatial reasoning with a transformer encoder for temporal modeling. A bidirectional cross-attention module enables mutual refinement of spatial and temporal features, while AU-guided dynamic connectivity adapts facial region interactions according to muscle activation patterns. The transformer captures subtle temporal dynamics beyond apex-based approaches, improving semantic consistency and interpretability for explainable micro-expression recognition. The fused representation is optimized using focal loss and evaluated on CASME II, 4DME, DFME, NaME, SAMM, and SMIC-HS. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved robustness, generalization, interpretability, and computational efficiency, confirming the effectiveness of adaptive relational reasoning, AU-guided dynamic connectivity, and deep spatial-temporal feature fusion for accurate cross-dataset micro-expression recognition.

</details>


### [68] [Directing the World: Fast Autoregressive Video Generation with Compositional Human-Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27964)
*Haoyuan Wang,Yabo Chen,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“Directing the World”框架，通过解耦控制学习与统一视频先验，结合Fast-Slow Memory训练策略、t-guided动态投影、Motion-CFG策略及两阶段相机轨迹控制模块，实现长时序、高可控、高视觉质量的视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建交互式世界模型需要生成真实视频并保持长时序可控动力学。自回归视频生成可扩展但存在误差累积和时序退化问题，尤其在异质控制（人体运动和相机轨迹）下更严重，现有方法常在可控性和视觉质量间权衡。

Method: 解耦控制学习同时保持统一自回归视频先验；引入Fast-Slow Memory训练策略稳定长时序学习；针对人体运动控制设计t-guided动态投影机制和Motion-CFG策略，实现平滑准确运动对齐且支持多人控制；学习运动先验后，引入第二阶段相机轨迹控制模块组合人体动力学与视角变化；构建大规模同步数据集，分为运动中心和相机中心子集用于解耦训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法在长时序生成上稳定，具备精确可控性和高视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过解耦控制学习和统一视频先验，结合多阶段控制模块和专门训练策略，实现了可控世界模型视频生成，平衡了可控性与视觉质量。

Abstract: Building interactive world models requires generating realistic videos while maintaining controllable dynamics over long horizons. Autoregressive video generation offers a scalable foundation, but suffers from error accumulation and temporal degradation during extended rollouts. This issue is further amplified under heterogeneous controls such as human motion and camera trajectories, which may interfere and destabilize a pretrained video prior, while existing methods often trade off controllability and visual quality. We propose "Directing the World", a fast autoregressive framework for controllable world-model video generation with compositional human-motion and camera-trajectory control. Our key idea is to decouple control learning while preserving a unified autoregressive video prior. We introduce a Fast-Slow Memory training strategy to stabilize long-horizon rollout learning and improve convergence. For human motion control, we design a t-guided Dynamic Projection mechanism and a refined Motion-CFG strategy, enabling temporally smooth and accurate motion alignment without degrading visual fidelity, and supporting multi-person control.After learning a robust motion prior, we introduce a second-stage camera-trajectory control module to compose human dynamics with viewpoint changes for coherent world exploration. We further construct a large-scale dataset with synchronized video, text, human-motion, and camera-trajectory annotations, organized into motion-centric and camera-centric subsets for decoupled training. Extensive experiments show stable long-horizon generation with precise controllability and high visual quality. See more at https://whydahuzi.github.io/Directing-the-World.github.io/.

</details>


### [69] [OSOR: One-Step Diffusion Inpainting for Effect-Aware Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28094)
*Qinming Zhou,Chenxi Sun,Deyang Kong,Junhao He,Xiangheng Tang,Peike Yu,Haotian Wu,Leilei Cao,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OSOR单步物体移除框架，通过占用引导判别器、Alpha头和SAVP验证流程实现高效且鲁棒的移除效果，在感知质量上超越多步扩散模型的同时速度提升4-30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在物体移除任务中存在两大瓶颈：1) 目标物体的非局部效应(阴影/反射)难以建模；2) 用户掩码常存在误差/不完整；3) 数十步去噪过程导致计算成本过高，限制交互场景应用。

Method: OSOR创新性地融合三项核心技术：① 占用引导判别器实现精确边界监督，支持单步扩散训练；② Alpha头利用预训练扩散模型知识预测最优移除区域，处理不完美掩码；③ 语义锚定验证流程(SAVP)过滤噪声指令三元组，构建大规模效果感知监督数据。配套构建28万对验证移除数据的CORNE数据集及两个专用评测基准。

Result: 实验表明OSOR在感知质量指标上显著优于强多步扩散基线模型，同时推理速度提升4-30倍。在AnimeEraseBench和TextEraseBench复杂任务中均取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: OSOR通过单步扩散架构与多模态验证机制的有效结合，首次实现了效果感知、掩码鲁棒且计算高效的物体移除，为实时图像编辑应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Real-world object removal is challenging due to two key difficulties: the target object's non-local effects, such as shadows and reflections, which are difficult to model, and the fact that user-provided masks are often inaccurate or incomplete. With billions of parameters and tens of denoising steps, diffusion-based models achieve strong removal performance at the expense of substantial computational cost, limiting their use in interactive applications and on edge devices. To address these challenges, we present OSOR (One-Step Object Removal), which simultaneously achieves efficient, effect-aware, and mask-robust object removal. Concretely, OSOR introduces: (1) an occupancy-guided discriminator for precise boundary supervision, enabling stable single-step diffusion training; (2) an alpha head that leverages knowledge from pretrained diffusion models to predict appropriate removal regions with minimal overhead, thereby handling imperfect masks; and (3) a semantic-anchored verification pipeline (SAVP) that filters noisy instruction-based triplets to produce effect-aware supervision at scale. Using SAVP, we curate CORNE, which contains 280K verified removal pairs, and further annotate AnimeEraseBench and TextEraseBench to evaluate performance on more complex removal tasks. Experiments show that OSOR surpasses strong multi-step diffusion baselines in perceptual quality while achieving $4\times$ to $30\times$ faster inference.

</details>


### [70] [BiDeMem: Bidirectional Degradation Memory for Explainable Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28112)
*Xinrui Wu,Lichen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiDeMem双向退化记忆模型，通过可解释的退化先验提升图像恢复性能，验证其在多退化场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像恢复方法依赖单一指标（如PSNR）评估，缺乏对退化先验可解释性的验证，难以区分模型是否真正理解退化机制而非利用数据捷径。

Method: 设计双向退化记忆模块：1) 基于恢复特征与输入统计构建查询，检索top-k记忆槽；2) 同一记忆槽同时支持推理时的恢复路径和训练时的前向退化解释路径；3) 在受控NAFNet框架下通过消融实验验证各组件贡献。

Result: 1) 对比校正头、密集查询先验、静态全局先验等变体，BiDeMem分别提升0.2588/0.2586/0.2839 dB；2) 强残差监督与宽退化头仍不及完整模型；3) 干预实验证明其提升错误先验/本土先验敏感性的同时保持恢复质量。

Conclusion: BiDeMem首次实现退化记忆的双重功能：既是性能提升模块，又是可证伪的解释机制，为图像恢复提供可验证的退化先验框架。

Abstract: Degradation-aware prompts, conditions, and latent priors are increasingly used in image restoration, yet they are usually judged by a single endpoint: whether the restored image obtains higher PSNR. This is a weak test of semantics. A condition can help by adding capacity, acting as a global correction bias, or exploiting dataset shortcuts, without becoming an interpretable degradation prior. We propose BiDeMem, a bidirectional degradation memory for explainable image restoration. A query built from restoration features and input statistics retrieves a compact top-k subset of memory slots. The same selected slot identity supports the restoration path at inference time and a training-only forward-degradation explanation path. The study centers on verifiability in a controlled multi-degradation NAFNet setting. New controls separate the gain from a correction head alone, a dense query prior, and a static global prior: these variants are 0.2588, 0.2586, and 0.2839 dB below BiRank, respectively. Strong residual supervision and a wider degradation head also remain below the full bidirectional memory model. Intervention probes show that BiRank preserves restoration quality while increasing wrong-prior and native-prior sensitivity, framing degradation memory as both a restoration module and a falsifiable explanation mechanism.

</details>


### [71] [Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning with Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27988)
*Xirui Teng,Nan Xi,Junsong Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LVDR框架，通过关键点引导的MCTS实现可解释的视觉推理，提升动作质量评估准确性并揭示决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作质量评估模型虽能准确评估表现，但作为黑箱系统缺乏对底层推理过程的显式揭示能力，限制了其可解释性和可信度。

Method: 设计Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning (LVDR)框架，融合关键点引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)技术，构建并可视化潜在视觉推理路径。

Result: 在4个跨体育与手术领域的数据集上验证，LVDR在保持竞争性定量性能的同时，生成可解释的视觉推理轨迹，显著提升评估透明度。

Conclusion: LVDR有效解决了动作质量评估中的黑箱问题，在提升评估准确性的同时实现推理过程的可视化，为细粒度技能分析提供可靠工具。

Abstract: Analyzing fine-grained skill activities (e.g., sports, surgery) requires not only recognizing visual patterns but also performing step-by-step visual reasoning that leads to the final judgment. While recent advances in action quality assessment have achieved remarkable progress in evaluating performance, existing models remain black boxes, where they lack the ability to explicitly reveal the reasoning processes underlying their judgments. To address this limitation, we propose Latent Visual Diffusion Reasoning (LVDR), a novel framework that integrates keypoint-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to model and visualize the latent visual reasoning process. LVDR not only produces more accurate skill assessments but also uncovers the critical visual reasoning sequences that contribute to the final evaluation. Extensive experiments across four datasets spanning diverse sports and surgical domains demonstrate that LVDR achieves competitive quantitative performance while providing interpretable visual reasoning trajectories leading to the final predictions. Source codes and models can be found through the following link: https://github.com/XiruiTeng/LVDR_Official.git.

</details>


### [72] [HumanMoveVQA: Can Video MLLMs reason about human movement in videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27999)
*Pulkit Gera,Faegheh Sardari,Asmar Nadeem,Valentina Bono,Padraig Boulton,Adrian Hilton,Armin Mustafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个评估外部视角下全局人体运动轨迹与方向推理的基准HumanMoveVQA，揭示现有模型能力缺陷并通过微调开源模型验证可学习性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs将复杂人体运动简化为粗粒度语义标签，现有基准缺乏对时空全局运动（轨迹/方向变化）的评估能力

Method: 构建基于首帧锚定世界坐标系的多阶段流水线，将2D视频观测提升为世界一致的3D运动轨迹，生成7类共10K+结构化问答对

Result: 发现主流专有模型存在显著能力差距，但通过针对性世界一致监督微调开源基线可实现性能突破

Conclusion: 建立了几何严谨的运动感知视频理解新范式，为下一代模型发展提供关键评估框架

Abstract: Despite the rapid advance of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in high-level video understanding, a fundamental bottleneck remains: these models collapse complex human motion into coarse semantic labels. Existing benchmarks mostly focus on scene-centric events or local joint articulations, failing to probe global human motion in space over time (trajectory and orientation changes). We introduce HumanMoveVQA, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate global trajectory and orientation reasoning from an exocentric perspective. Our benchmark utilizes a first-frame anchored world coordinate system, preserving translation and rotation relative to a fixed starting point. We propose a scalable, multi-stage pipeline that lifts 2D video observations into world-consistent 3D motion tracks to generate over 10K structured question-answer pairs across seven reasoning categories, including motion aggregation, sequential ordering, and trajectory-level inference. Our extensive evaluation reveals a critical capability gap in state-of-the-art proprietary models on deep human motion understanding. However, we demonstrate that this is a learnable problem; by fine-tuning an open-source baseline with our targeted, world-consistent supervision, we achieve a significant improvement.HumanMoveVQA establishes a rigorous geometric foundation for developing next-generation, movement-aware video understanding models.

</details>


### [73] [Toward Robust In-Context Segmentation via Concept Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28149)
*Zhigang Chen,Xiawu Zheng,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出概念引导的上下文分割（CG-ICS）范式，通过高层语义概念提取和视觉示例并行路径，显著提升上下文分割任务的鲁棒性和准确率，实现跨参考图像的稳定分割结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文分割（ICS）研究普遍忽视系统鲁棒性问题，即同一查询在不同参考图像下分割结果的一致性，导致模型可靠性不足。

Method: 提出CG-ICS框架：1）概念推理模块利用多模态大语言模型生成候选文本概念，结合SAM3评分函数和树搜索优化筛选可靠概念；2）并行视觉示例路径通过上下文构建提供查询侧空间定位；3）双路径信息共同激活冻结的SAM3主干网络完成分割。

Result: 在标准ICS基准测试中达到SOTA准确率，同时显著降低不同参考选择下的结果方差，验证了鲁棒性提升效果。

Conclusion: CG-ICS通过概念引导机制有效解决了ICS系统的鲁棒性问题，为构建高可靠性的上下文分割系统提供了新范式，在准确性和稳定性方面均实现突破。

Abstract: In-context segmentation (ICS) requires a model to segment target regions in a query image using only a few reference images and their corresponding masks, without updating any parameters.
